- **Verdict: Does NOT deserve S-tier.** Core loop is always "wait → react → press Enter." Modifiers (FOG, SILENCE, QUICK DRAW, MIRROR, DUST, DOUBLE BLUFF) add variety but not depth. SILENCE is genuinely clever (flips core mechanic) but the strategic decision space remains binary (shoot/don't shoot). Session Variance: 3 is honest — compare to Dungeon Crawl (dozens of decisions per session) or Trader (multiple commodities, news, black swans). (src: memory/2026-04-04.md:12)
